# ex_28 -- Figure 2 (model comparison: gradient boosting, KNN, random forest, NN; fixed + tuned)
# Paper: Figure 2 (main text, fig:ex01); Supplementary Section S5.1, Tables S3 (fixed settings) and S5 (search spaces)
# Source: graph_hdc revision/consolidated, _slurm_ex_28.py regenerated in a copy (mode main, with no caches/archives:
#         all HDF warmups + all 36 studies + all 90 fixed runs); opteval rebuilt with opteval_commands() from the best
#         configurations _ex27/jupiter/ex28/ex28_configs.csv (graph_hdc 512b141; journals stayed on JUPITER).
#         Archive prefixes: ex_27_warmup, ex_28_fixed, ex_28_trial__..., ex_28_opt. JUPITER jobs 2420640, 2423838 (main),
#         2426678, 2427761, 2455606 (opteval).
# Runs: primer 2, warmup 46, main 126, opteval 360
#       main = 36 Optuna studies (3 models x 3 representations x 4 targets; 12 random forest, 12 KNN, 12 GB) + 90 fixed
#       runs (3 dataset groups x 3 representations x 10 seeds; RF and GB in one run, QM9 U0+gap via TARGET_INDICES);
#       opteval = 36 best configurations x 10 seeds (120 RF + 240 GB/KNN)
# Protocol: Crippen logP (mixin_clogp), AqSolDB logS, QM9 U0 and gap; HDF (standard encoder, CODEBOOK_SEED 0), Morgan,
#   RDKit (2048, radius 2) as in ex_27; seeds 0-9; fixed RF 100 trees/depth 10/sqrt, GB 100 est./lr 0.01/depth 3
#   (+ subsample 0.75, sqrt features); tuning: 30 DISTINCT configurations of TPE per study on seed 420 (full data),
#   objective validation MAE; RF {10,100,500} x depth {None,10,20} x features {sqrt,0.1}; GB {10,100,300} x depth
#   {2,3,4} x lr {0.01,0.1,0.2}; KNN k {3,5,10} x weights {uniform,distance}, native metric, torch backend; plus the
#   ex_27 representation spaces. RF_NUM_JOBS (20 here) is an execution setting only (paper: 20/40/60 threads).
# Analysis: python analyze_ex_28.py   (-> _ex27/ex28/fig2_runs.csv, fig2_summary.csv, ex28_configs.csv, fig2_report.txt)
#           python make_figure_model_comparison.py _ex27/ex28/fig2_runs.csv figure_model_comparison.pdf --png
# Cost: JUPITER whole nodes; main ~2 x 4 h node jobs (an HDF QM9 RF trial at 500 trees / 8192 dims ~1.5-2 h on 20
#   threads, a 300-tree GB trial ~50 min); opteval ~4 h + 6 h node jobs (QM9 RF runs); memory: keep concurrent QM9
#   runs within ~450 GB. Of the order of 10^4 core-hours on CPU.
# Notes: REQUIRES ex_27 (protocol ex_27.txt): the NN bars (fixed + tuned) and the fixed KNN bars are read from the
#   ex_27_fixed and ex_27_opt archives, analyze_ex_28.py needs the ex_27 journals (_ex27/optuna_full) and checks that
#   every ex_28 test split equals the ex_27 one (so run on the same dataset caches). The warmup lines write the 48
#   HDF encodings (qm9_smiles, aqsoldb) that the trials can request; after ex_27 they already exist (the paper
#   generated no ex_28 warmups). opteval lines are the configurations the paper selected; after re-running the
#   studies regenerate them with "python _slurm_ex_28.py opteval". Datasets via chem_mat_data (qm9_smiles, aqsoldb).
# @stage primer
python predict_molecules__hdc.py --__DEBUG__="False" --__PREFIX__="'ex_27_warmup'" --SEED="0" --NUM_TEST="0.1" --NUM_TRAIN="1.0" --NUM_DATA="1.0" --DATASET_NAME="'aqsoldb'" --DATASET_TYPE="'regression'" --NN_NUM_WORKERS="0" --NOTE="'aqsoldb_logs'" --EMBEDDING_SIZE="8192" --NUM_LAYERS="1" --ENCODING_MODE="'continuous'" --SPECTRUM="'unit'" --BIDIRECTIONAL="True" --HYDROGEN_COUNT="'total'" --GRAPH_ATTRIBUTES="False" --CODEBOOK_SEED="0" --DEVICE="'cpu'" --MODELS="[]"
python predict_molecules__hdc.py --__DEBUG__="False" --__PREFIX__="'ex_27_warmup'" --SEED="0" --NUM_TEST="0.1" --NUM_TRAIN="1.0" --NUM_DATA="1.0" --DATASET_NAME="'qm9_smiles'" --DATASET_TYPE="'regression'" --NN_NUM_WORKERS="0" --NOTE="'qm9_energy'" --TARGET_INDEX="10" --EMBEDDING_SIZE="8192" --NUM_LAYERS="1" --ENCODING_MODE="'continuous'" --SPECTRUM="'unit'" --BIDIRECTIONAL="True" --HYDROGEN_COUNT="'total'" --GRAPH_ATTRIBUTES="False" --CODEBOOK_SEED="0" --DEVICE="'cpu'" --MODELS="[]"
# @stage warmup
